## User (or harness) — 2026-11-23 20:41:25 UTC

Fill gaps in the IHP sg13cmos5l slot-level characterization of a taped-out analog slot (slot 7: Banba bandgap `bg_core_pd` → `bias_dist_pd` → replica bias `pad_ref_rb` (vcm ≈1.26 V, vref ≈0.49 V) and two custom pads S2D/D2S; glue `s7_ctrl` with modes default/swap/mon/feed/ext, key dig_in[3]=1, dig_in[4]=0). Known open item: in the designer's tier-1 mode runs, at ss / −40 °C the modes swap, mon, feed, ext, swap+feed FAILED (no DC result; the stall is at the bandgap start-up node), and swap/mon stall in transient; default/all_high/no_key_swap pass. Also only 5 PVT points (tt27/3.3, ss-40/3.0, ff125/3.6, fs125/3.6, sf-40/3.0) were run, and `bg_core_pd` has no characterization of its own (only inside other suites).

Tasks (priority order; time box ~60 min total; check `date -u` and stop by 22:15 UTC at the latest; write partial results as you go):
1. Re-run the designer's tier-1 mode simulation (`docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/run_modes.py`, `run_tier1.py` and netlists at the design-repo commit below) and resolve the ss/−40 °C failures by simulator means that do not change the circuit (e.g. transient power-up from 0 V with the enable sequence, then operating point from the transient end; `.nodeset`/`.ic` only as diagnosis), and report per mode whether the CIRCUIT starts and settles to the expected output (expected D2S out = vcm + (in − vref); swap: vcm − (in − vref)) or whether there is a genuine start-up problem (a second stable state of the bandgap). 2. Extend the mode table to all 5 corners × −40/27/125 °C at 3.0/3.6 V where cheap (or at least all 5 corners at −40 and 125 °C).
3. `bg_core_pd` alone: output current (sourced into an NMOS diode or 0.7 V) over 5 MOS corners × resistor corners if available × −40/27/125 °C × 3.0/3.3/3.6 V; start-up by supply ramp (fast and slow ramp, e.g. 1 µs and 1 ms) at the extremes; supply sensitivity. Use the schematic netlist (and the extracted one if it exists at the commit).

Environment (installed): IHP PDK /foss/pdks/ihp-sg13cmos5l (PDK_ROOT=/foss/pdks PDK=ihp-sg13cmos5l), ngspice with IHP OSDI models, CACE 2.11, magic, netgen, KLayout. 4 CPUs shared with two other jobs: at most 2 parallel ngspice runs.

**Tool call: `Write`**

file: `/home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/slot_modes_bg/bg_core.py`

```
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""bg_core_pd alone (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation): output current over corners, supply ramps, supply sensitivity.

usage: python3 -I bg_core.py <design-repo-tree> <work-dir> <out-dir> [dc|ramp|all]

<design-repo-tree> holds macros/bg_core_pd/netlist/{schematic,pex} at the design-repo commit (run.sh fetches them).
Views: schematic = netlist/schematic/bg_core_pd_magic.spice (subckt bg_core_pd), extracted =
netlist/pex/bg_core_pd_magic_pex_1.spice (subckt bg_core_pd_pex, same port order vdd vss iout en_3v3 en_3v3_b).

Load: iout into a 0.7 V source (I reported = current sourced by the core). Enabled: en_3v3 = vdd, en_3v3_b = 0.
DC: operating point from a 2 us transient ramp of vdd (0 -> V, en tied to vdd) and its end state, cross-checked with a
plain .op; MOS tt/ss/ff/sf/fs x rhigh res_typ/res_bcs/res_wcs x -40/27/125 C x 3.0/3.3/3.6 V, pnpMPA typ.
Ramp: vdd and en_3v3 ramp 0 -> V in 1 us and in 1 ms, iout vs time, at the four extreme MOS corners and both T ends.
"""
import csv
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from pathlib import Path

TREE, WORK, OUT = (Path(a).resolve() for a in sys.argv[1:4])
WHAT = sys.argv[4] if len(sys.argv) > 4 else "all"
BG = TREE / "macros" / "bg_core_pd" / "netlist"
VIEWS = {"schematic": (BG / "schematic" / "bg_core_pd_magic.spice", "bg_core_pd"),
         "extracted": (BG / "pex" / "bg_core_pd_magic_pex_1.spice", "bg_core_pd_pex")}
MOS = ["tt", "ss", "ff", "sf", "fs"]
RES = ["res_typ", "res_bcs", "res_wcs"]
TEMPS = [-40, 27, 125]
VDDS = [3.0, 3.3, 3.6]
NPAR = 2


def head(view, mos, res, t):
    f, cell = VIEWS[view]
    return f"""* bg_core_pd {view} {mos} {res} {t} C
.lib cornerMOShv.lib mos_{mos}
.lib cornerRES.lib {res}
.lib cornerPNP.lib typ
.include {f}
.temp {t}
.option gmin=1e-12
.param vdd=3.3
Vdd vdd 0 {{vdd}}
Ven en 0 {{vdd}}
Venb enb 0 0
Vload iout 0 0.7
X1 vdd 0 iout en enb {cell}
"""


def run(deck, name):
    f = WORK / f"{name}.spice"
    f.write_text(deck)
    log = subprocess.run(["ngspice", "-b", f.name], cwd=WORK, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
    f.with_suffix(".log").write_text(log)
    return log


def dc_job(view, mos, res, t):
    # per V: plain op, then a 2 us supply ramp from 0 V (en tied to vdd) and its end values
    ctl = []
    for v in VDDS:
        ctl.append(f"""alter Vdd pwl(0 0 2u {v} 1 {v})
alter Ven pwl(0 0 2u {v} 1 {v})
tran 2n 30u uic
let iend = i(Vload)[length(i(Vload))-1]
let gend = v(x1.g)[length(v(x1.g))-1]
alter Vdd dc {v}
alter Ven dc {v}
op
let iop = i(Vload)
echo "RES {v} $&iend $&iop $&gend"
destroy all""")
    deck = head(view, mos, res, t) + ".control\n" + "\n".join(ctl) + "\n.endc\n.end\n"
    if view == "extracted":
        deck = deck.replace("v(x1.g)", "v(x1.bg_n4_0.g)")
    log = run(deck, f"bgdc_{view}_{mos}_{res}_{t}")
    rows = []
    for m in re.finditer(r"^RES (\S+) (\S+) (\S+) (\S+)", log, re.M):
        v, iend, iop, g = (float(x) for x in m.groups())
        rows.append(dict(view=view, mos=mos, res=res, temp=t, vdd=v, i_tran_uA=round(iend * 1e6, 4),
                         i_op_uA=round(iop * 1e6, 4), v_g_V=round(g, 4)))
    if len(rows) != len(VDDS):
        rows.append(dict(view=view, mos=mos, res=res, temp=t, vdd="FAILED", i_tran_uA="", i_op_uA="", v_g_V=""))
    return rows


def ramp_job(view, mos, t, v, tr):
    tstop = {"1u": "40u", "1m": "1.2m"}[tr]
    tstep = {"1u": "10n", "1m": "1u"}[tr]
    deck = head(view, mos, "res_typ", t).replace(f"Vdd vdd 0 {{vdd}}", f"Vdd vdd 0 pwl(0 0 {tr} {v})").replace(
        "Ven en 0 {vdd}", f"Ven en 0 pwl(0 0 {tr} {v})")
    g = "x1.g" if view == "schematic" else "x1.bg_n4_0.g"
    name = f"bgramp_{view}_{mos}_{t}_{v}_{tr}"
    deck += f""".control
tran {tstep} {tstop} uic
let iu = i(Vload)*1e6
meas tran iend find iu at={tstop}
let thr = 0.9*iend
meas tran t90 when iu=thr cross=last
wrdata {name}.dat iu v(vdd) v({g})
echo "RAMP $&iend $&t90"
.endc
.
… [1624 more characters cut]
```

cd /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/slot_modes_bg && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='bg_core.py'; s=open(p).read()
old_start=s.index("def dc_job(")
old_end=s.index("def ramp_job(")
new='''def dc_job(view, mos, res, t):
    # per V (own deck): 2 us supply ramp from 0 V (en tied to vdd), values at 100 / 199 us; then a plain op
    g = "x1.g" if view == "schematic" else "x1.bg_n4_0.g"
    rows = []
    for v in VDDS:
        deck = head(view, mos, res, t).replace("Vdd vdd 0 {vdd}", f"Vdd vdd 0 pwl(0 0 2u {v})").replace(
            "Ven en 0 {vdd}", f"Ven en 0 pwl(0 0 2u {v})") + f""".control
tran 50n 200u uic
meas tran iend find i(Vload) at=199u
meas tran i100 find i(Vload) at=100u
meas tran gend find v({g}) at=199u
echo "TRAN $&iend $&i100 $&gend"
destroy all
alter Vdd dc {v}
alter Ven dc {v}
op
let iop = i(Vload)
echo "OP $&iop"
.endc
.end
"""
        log = run(deck, f"bgdc_{view}_{mos}_{res}_{t}_{v}")
        a = re.search(r"^TRAN (\\S+) (\\S+) (\\S+)", log, re.M)
        b = re.search(r"^OP (\\S+)", log, re.M)
        if not (a and b):
            rows.append(dict(view=view, mos=mos, res=res, temp=t, vdd=v, i_tran_uA="FAILED", i_tran100_uA="",
                             i_op_uA="", v_g_V=""))
            continue
        iend, i100, gv = (float(x) for x in a.groups())
        rows.append(dict(view=view, mos=mos, res=res, temp=t, vdd=v, i_tran_uA=round(iend * 1e6, 4),
                         i_tran100_uA=round(i100 * 1e6, 4), i_op_uA=round(float(b.group(1)) * 1e6, 4),
                         v_g_V=round(gv, 4)))
    return rows


'''
s=s[:old_start]+new+s[old_end:]
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

## Assistant — 2026-11-23 20:47:16 UTC

**Tool call: `Write`**

file: `/home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/slot_modes_bg/slot_modes.py`

```
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Slot 7 modes from power-up (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation): transient power-up, enable, mode bits, pad-in staircase.

usage: python3 -I slot_modes.py <design-repo-tree> <work-dir> <out-dir> <job> [<job> ...]
  job = <mode>:<mos>:<temp>:<vdd>[:<variant>]   e.g. swap:ss:-40:3.0
  variant: "pu" (default; power-up transient, see below), "dcop" (the designer's DC sweep as in run_modes.py, stderr kept)

Same netlists and testbench as the designer's docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/run_modes.py at the design-repo commit (it is
imported from there, via run_tier1.wrapped(), so the decks use the same views): both custom pads, pad_ref_rb and
bias_dist_pd from their extracted layout netlists; bg_core_pd from its schematic netlist; ls_up_fs / s7_ctrl / hv_logic
/ en_ctrl_3v3 from their source netlists. MOS corner on hv and lv, res_typ, pnp typ.

"pu": vdd_3v3 and iovdd ramp 0 -> V in 10 us; v12 (1.2 V) and the mode bits d0..d5 are there from t = 0 (the harness
writes the register before enabling), enable 0 -> 1.2 V at 20 us (100 ns edge). Pad in 0.5 V until 200 us, then a
staircase 0 / 0.25 / 0.5 / 0.75 / 1.0 V (40 us each, 200 ... 400 us). Feed modes: bus0 / bus1 through 10 kohm to 1.45 /
1.05 V (as run_modes.py), pad in fixed at 0.5 V. ext: bus2 / bus3 through 10 kohm to 1.30 / 0.50 V. Error =
pad out - expected at the end of each step (expected as in run_modes.py). No .nodeset / .ic: plain transient with uic
from all-zero (every node starts at 0 V), i.e. no circuit change and no forced state.
"""
import csv
import re
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

TREE, WORK, OUT = (Path(a).resolve() for a in sys.argv[1:4])
JOBS = sys.argv[4:]
T1 = TREE / "docs" / "slot7_hookup" / "tier1"
sys.path.insert(0, str(T1))
import run_tier1 as T  # noqa: E402

MODES = {"default": ("0 0 0 0 0 0", "direct"), "swap": ("1 0 0 1 0 0", "crossed"), "mon": ("0 1 0 1 0 0", "direct"),
         "feed": ("0 0 1 1 0 0", "feed"), "ext": ("0 0 0 1 0 1", "ext"), "all_high": ("1 1 1 1 1 1", "direct"),
         "swap+feed": ("1 0 1 1 0 0", "feed"), "no_key_swap": ("1 0 0 0 0 0", "direct")}
EXP = {"direct": "v(vcm_s2d) + v(padin) - v(vref_s2d)", "crossed": "v(vcm_s2d) - v(padin) + v(vref_s2d)",

```
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
#
# Reproduce everything in this directory (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation, ngspice + IHP OSDI models).
# usage: ./run.sh [WORKDIR]   (default WORKDIR: ./_work, not committed; outputs written next to this script)
# Steps (each can be run alone: ./run.sh WORKDIR fetch|designer|modes|bg|tables)
set -euo pipefail
HERE="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)"
WORK="$(mkdir -p "${1:-$HERE/_work}" && cd "${1:-$HERE/_work}" && pwd)"
STEP="${2:-all}"
REPO_URL=https://github.com/tatzelbrumm/sg13cmos5l_cm_ip__single2diff2single
REPO_COMMIT=9052bd35928c6d40344e8e686c9abbe736bfadb9
export PDK_ROOT=${PDK_ROOT:-/foss/pdks} PDK=${PDK:-ihp-sg13cmos5l}
SPICEINIT="$PDK_ROOT/$PDK/libs.tech/ngspice/.spiceinit"
[ -f "$SPICEINIT" ] || SPICEINIT=/foss/pdks/IHP-Open-PDK/ihp-sg13cmos5l/libs.tech/ngspice/.spiceinit
TREE="$WORK/tree"
NPAR=2   # at most 2 ngspice runs in parallel

fetch() {
  # only the needed paths of the design repository, at the commit (no full clone)
  if [ ! -d "$WORK/git" ]; then
    git clone -q --depth 1 --filter=blob:none --no-checkout "$REPO_URL" "$WORK/git"
  fi
  git -C "$WORK/git" fetch -q --depth 1 origin "$REPO_COMMIT"
  rm -rf "$TREE"; mkdir -p "$TREE"
  git -C "$WORK/git" archive FETCH_HEAD docs/slot7_hookup/tier1 macros/bg_core_pd/netlist macros/slot_7/netlist \
    macros/pad_ref_rb/netlist macros/bias_dist_pd/netlist macros/en_ctrl_3v3/netlist macros/hv_logic/netlist \
    macros/en_ctrl_3v3/testbenches/spice/run_starve.py macros/sg13cmos5l_IOPadSingle2Diff/netlist \
    macros/sg13cmos5l_IOPadDiff2Single/netlist | tar x -C "$TREE"
  mkdir -p "$TREE/docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/runs"
  for d in "$TREE/docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/runs" "$WORK/slot" "$WORK/bg"; do mkdir -p "$d"; cp "$SPICEINIT" "$d/.spiceinit"; done
}

designer() {
  # the designer's own run_modes.py, unchanged, at all three of its points (writes modes.txt in the fetched tree)
  (cd "$TREE/docs/slot7_hookup/tier1" && python3 -I run_modes.py)
  cp "$TREE/docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/modes.txt" "$HERE/designer_modes_rerun.txt"
}

modes() {
  rm -f "$HERE"/slot_modes_*.csv.raw
  # job list: see jobs_modes.txt (one job per line, <mode>:<mos>:<temp>:<vdd>[:variant])
  grep -v '^#' "$HERE/jobs_modes.txt" | xargs -P "$NPAR" -n 1 \
    python3 -I "$HERE/slot_modes.py" "$TREE" "$WORK/slot" "$WORK/slot_out"
}

bg() {
  python3 -I "$HERE/bg_core.py" "$TREE" "$WORK/bg" "$HERE" all
}

tables() {
  python3 -I "$HERE/tables.py" "$WORK" "$HERE"
}

case "$STEP" in
  all) fetch; designer; modes; bg; tables ;;
  *) "$STEP" ;;
esac
```

# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Plots from the wrdata files (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation).

usage: python3 -I plots.py <work-dir> <out-dir>
  <work-dir>/slot/pu_<mode>_<mos>_<temp>_<vdd>.dat  (slot_modes.py, power-up transient)
  <work-dir>/bg/bgramp_<view>_<mos>_<temp>_<vdd>_<ramp>.dat (bg_core.py)
"""
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import matplotlib
matplotlib.use("Agg")
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt  # noqa: E402
import numpy as np  # noqa: E402

WORK, OUT = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
SLOT_SEL = [("swap", "ss", -40, 3.0), ("mon", "ss", -40, 3.0), ("default", "ss", -40, 3.0)]


def load(f):
    a = np.loadtxt(f)
    return a[:, 0], a[:, 1::2]


def slot_plot():
    files = [(s, WORK / "slot" / f"pu_{s[0]}_{s[1]}_{s[2]}_{s[3]}.dat") for s in SLOT_SEL]
    files = [(s, f) for s, f in files if f.exists()]
    if not files:
        return
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(3, 1, figsize=(8, 8), sharex=True)
    for (mode, c, t, v), f in files:
        tt, y = load(f)
        tt = tt * 1e6
        ax[0].plot(tt, y[:, 0], label=f"{mode}: bandgap g")
        ax[0].plot(tt, y[:, 4], "--", label=f"{mode}: ibg (bias_dist_pd in)")
        ax[1].plot(tt, y[:, 5], label=f"{mode}: vcm")
        ax[1].plot(tt, y[:, 6], "--", label=f"{mode}: vref")
        ax[1].plot(tt, y[:, 7], ":", label=f"{mode}: D2S pad out")
        ax[2].plot(tt, y[:, 9], label=f"{mode}: I(vdd_3v3)")
    ax[0].set_ylabel("V")
    ax[1].set_ylabel("V")
    ax[2].set_ylabel("uA")
    ax[2].set_xlabel("time [us]  (supplies 0->V 0..10 us, enable at 20 us, pad-in steps from 200 us)")
    for a in ax:
        a.grid(alpha=0.3)
        a.legend(fontsize=6, ncol=3)
    ax[2].set_ylim(-20, 200)
    fig.suptitle("IHP sg13cmos5l simulation: slot 7 power-up, ss / -40 C / 3.0 V\n(bg_core_pd schematic; pads, "
                 "pad_ref_rb, bias_dist_pd extracted)", fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(OUT / "slot_powerup_ss_m40.png", dpi=90)


def bg_plot():
    fs = sorted((WORK / "bg").glob("bgramp_schematic_*.dat"))
    if not fs:
        return
    fig, ax = plt.subplots(1, 2, figsize=(10, 4))
    for f in fs:
        _, _, mos, t, v, tr = f.stem.split("_")
        tt, y = load(f)
        k = 0 if tr == "1u" else 1
        ax[k].plot(tt * (1e6 if k == 0 else 1e3), y[:, 0], label=f"{mos} {t} C {v} V", lw=0.8)
    ax[0].set_xlabel("time [us] (vdd = en_3v3 ramp 0->V in 1 us)")
    ax[1].set_xlabel("time [ms] (vdd = en_3v3 ramp 0->V in 1 ms)")
    for a in ax:
        a.set_ylabel("iout into 0.7 V [uA]")
        a.grid(alpha=0.3)
        a.legend(fontsize=6)
    fig.suptitle("IHP sg13cmos5l simulation: bg_core_pd (schematic) start-up by supply ramp", fontsize=9)
    fig.tight_layout()
    fig.savefig(OUT / "bg_core_ramp.png", dpi=90)


slot_plot()
bg_plot()
```

p='bg_core.py'; s=open(p).read()
s=s.replace('''def write_csv(rows, path):''','''def diag_job(view, mos, t, v):
    """Is there a second (zero-current) state? (a) .op with .nodeset at the off state; (b) transient with vdd at its
    final value and .ic at the off state (g = 0, vpg = vpc = ks = vdd, iout node held by the load)."""
    pre = "x1." if view == "schematic" else "x1.bg_n4_0."
    vpg = "x1.vpg" if view == "schematic" else "x1.bg_pmir_pcas_c_0.vpg"
    off = f"v({pre}g)=0 v({vpg})={v} v({pre}vpc)={v} v({pre}ks)={v}"
    base = head(view, mos, "res_typ", t).replace("Vdd vdd 0 {vdd}", f"Vdd vdd 0 {v}").replace(
        "Ven en 0 {vdd}", f"Ven en 0 {v}")
    deck = base + f""".nodeset {off}
.ic {off}
.control
op
let iop = i(Vload)
let gop = v({pre}g)
echo "NSOP $&iop $&gop"
destroy all
tran 10n 50u uic
meas tran ifin find i(Vload) at=49u
let thr = 0.9*ifin
meas tran t90 when i(Vload)=thr cross=1
meas tran g0 find v({pre}g) at=0
echo "ICTR $&ifin $&t90 $&g0"
.endc
.end
"""
    name = f"bgdiag_{view}_{mos}_{t}_{v}"
    log = run(deck, name)
    a = re.search(r"^NSOP (\\S+) (\\S+)", log, re.M)
    b = re.search(r"^ICTR (\\S+) (\\S+) (\\S+)", log, re.M)
    r = dict(view=view, mos=mos, temp=t, vdd=v)
    r["i_op_nodeset_off_uA"] = round(float(a.group(1)) * 1e6, 4) if a else "FAILED"
    r["v_g_op_V"] = round(float(a.group(2)), 4) if a else ""
    r["i_tran_ic_off_50us_uA"] = round(float(b.group(1)) * 1e6, 4) if b else "FAILED"
    r["t90_from_off_us"] = round(float(b.group(2)) * 1e6, 3) if b else ""
    r["v_g_t0_V"] = round(float(b.group(3)), 4) if b else ""
    return r


def write_csv(rows, path):''')
s=s.replace('''        write_csv(rows, OUT / "bg_core_ramp.csv")''','''        write_csv(rows, OUT / "bg_core_ramp.csv")
    if WHAT in ("diag", "all"):
        jobs = [(vw, m, t, v) for vw in VIEWS for m in MOS for (t, v) in ((-40, 3.0), (125, 3.6))
                if vw == "schematic" or m in ("ss", "ff")]
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(NPAR) as ex:
            rows = list(ex.map(lambda a: diag_job(*a), jobs))
        write_csv(rows, OUT / "bg_core_diag.csv")''')
s=s.replace('''Ramp: vdd and en_3v3 ramp 0 -> V in 1 us and in 1 ms, iout vs time, at the four extreme MOS corners and both T ends.''','''Ramp: vdd and en_3v3 ramp 0 -> V in 1 us and in 1 ms, iout vs time, at the five MOS corners at -40 C / 3.0 V and

usage of the last argument: dc | ramp | diag | all''')

EOF

file: `/home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/slot_modes_bg/tables.py`

```
# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Tabulate slot_modes.py and bg_core.py results into results.md and CSVs (IHP sg13cmos5l simulation).

usage: python3 -I tables.py <work-dir> <out-dir>
  reads <work-dir>/slot_out/slot_modes_{pu,dcop,dcns}.csv (key=value rows) and <out-dir>/bg_core_{dc,ramp,diag}.csv
  writes <out-dir>/slot_modes_powerup.csv, <out-dir>/slot_modes_dc_diag.csv, <out-dir>/results_tables.md
"""
import csv
import statistics
import sys
from pathlib import Path

WORK, OUT = Path(sys.argv[1]), Path(sys.argv[2])
HDR = "# SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier\n# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0\n"
MODES = ["default", "swap", "mon", "feed", "ext", "all_high", "swap+feed", "no_key_swap"]


def kv_rows(f):
    rows = []
    if not f.exists():
        return rows
    for r in csv.reader(f.open()):
        d = {}
        for cell in r:
            k, _, v = cell.partition("=")
            d[k] = v
        rows.append(d)
    return rows


def write(rows, path, note):
    if not rows:
        return
    keys = list(dict.fromkeys(k for r in rows for k in r))
    with path.open("w", newline="") as f:
        f.write(HDR + f"# IHP sg13cmos5l simulation. {note}\n")
        w = csv.DictWriter(f, fieldnames=keys)
        w.writeheader()
        w.writerows(rows)


def read_csv(path):
    if not path.exists():
        return []
    return list(csv.DictReader(ln for ln in path.open() if not ln.startswith("#")))


def fnum(x):
    try:
        return float(x)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


md = []
# ---------------- slot modes from power-up
pu = kv_rows(WORK / "slot_out" / "slot_modes_pu.csv")
seen = {}
for r in pu:  # last run of a job wins
    seen[(r["mode"], r["mos"], r["temp"], r["vdd"])] = r
pu = list(seen.values())
write(pu, OUT / "slot_modes_powerup.csv", "slot 7 modes from power-up (slot_modes.py 'pu'); bg_core_pd schematic, "
      "pads / pad_ref_rb / bias_dist_pd extracted. err_max_mV = max |pad out - expected| over the pad-in steps; "
      "t_* absolute (enable at 20 us)")
pts = sorted({(r["mos"], r["temp"], r["vdd"]) for r in pu}, key=lambda p: (int(p[1]), p[0], float(p[2])))
md.append("### Slot 7 modes from power-up: max |D2S pad out - expected| [mV]\n")
md.append("IHP sg13cmos5l simulation; bg_core_pd schematic, both pads / pad_ref_rb / bias_dist_pd extracted, "
          "ls_up_fs / s7_ctrl source. Pad in 0 / 0.25 / 0.5 / 0.75 / 1.0 V (feed modes: 0.5 V). "
          "`FAILED` = simulator gave no result; empty = not run in the time box.\n")
md.append("| MOS | T [C] | V | " + " | ".join(MODES) + " | vcm / vref [V] | bandgap g [V] | t(g 90 %) after enable [us] |")
md.append("|---|---|---|" + "---|" * len(MODES) + "---|---|---|")
for p in pts:
    cells, vv, gg, tg = [], "", "", ""
    for m in MODES:
        r = seen.get((m, *p))
        cells.append("" if r is None else str(r.get("err_max_mV", "")))
        if r is not None and fnum(r.get("vcm_V")) is not None and not vv:
            vv = f"{float(r['vcm_V']):.3f} / {float(r['vref_V']):.3f}"
            gg = f"{float(r['v_g_V']):.3f}"
    tgs = [fnum(seen[(m, *p)].get("t_g90_us")) for m in MODES if (m, *p) in seen]
    tgs = [x - 20.0 for x in tgs if x is not None]
    tg = f"{min(tgs):.2f} ... {max(tgs):.2f}" if tgs else ""
    md.append(f"| {p[0]} | {p[1]} | {p[2]} | " + " | ".join(cells) + f" | {vv} | {gg} | {tg} |")
md.append("")

# ---------------- DC diagnosis
dd = kv_rows(WORK / "slot_out" / "slot_modes_dcop.csv") + kv_rows(WORK / "slot_out" / "slot_modes_dcns.csv")
write(dd, OUT / "slot_modes_dc_diag.csv", "the designer's DC deck (dcop) and the same with .nodeset of the bandgap nodes"
      " (dcns), ss -40 C 3.0 V; solver_notes = ngspice messages")
if dd:
    md.append("### DC solver diagnosis at ss / -40 C / 3.0 V (designer's DC deck)\n")
    md.append("| mode | variant | max error [mV] | ngspice (first failing instance, if any) |")
    md.append("|---|---|---|---|")
    
… [4820 more characters cut]

<!--
SPDX-FileCopyrightText: 2026 Christoph Maier
SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
-->
# Slot 7: mode start-up at ss / −40 °C, mode table over PVT, bg_core_pd alone

IHP sg13cmos5l simulation (ngspice, IHP OSDI models). This fills three gaps in the slot-level characterization of the
taped-out slot 7 (commit `158afc5afab0056bd6d216a4db3833e34a364949` of this repository):

1. **The ss / −40 °C mode failures** in the designer's tier-1 mode runs (`docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/modes.txt` of the
   design repository: swap, mon, feed, ext, swap+feed `FAILED` at ss −40 °C, "stall at the bandgap's start-up node").
   Question: simulator artefact or a second stable state of the bandgap (real start-up risk)?
2. **Mode table over more PVT points** than the designer's 3 (modes) / 5 (round trip).
3. **`bg_core_pd` alone**: output current over MOS × rhigh corners × T × V, start-up by supply ramp, supply
   sensitivity, and a check for a zero-current state.

Verdict and numbers: **`results.md`**. Generated tables: `results_tables.md`, CSVs, plots.

## Sources

| what | where |
|---|---|
| design repository | <https://github.com/tatzelbrumm/sg13cmos5l_cm_ip__single2diff2single> commit `9052bd35928c6d40344e8e686c9abbe736bfadb9` (fetched by `run.sh`, only the paths below, no full clone) |
| tier-1 scripts | `docs/slot7_hookup/tier1/run_tier1.py`, `run_modes.py` (imported unchanged: same netlists, same port wrapping) |
| slot glue | `macros/slot_7/netlist/source/slot_7.spice` (`slot_7`, `s7_ctrl`, `s7_doutpd`) |
| views used in the slot decks | both custom pads, `pad_ref_rb`, `bias_dist_pd`: **extracted** (`netlist/layout/*_klayout.cir`, converted by the designer's `run_starve.convert()`); `bg_core_pd`: **schematic** (`netlist/schematic/bg_core_pd_magic.spice`), as in the designer's runs; `ls_up_fs`, `s7_ctrl`, `hv_logic`, `en_ctrl_3v3`: source netlists |
| views used for bg_core_pd alone | **schematic** `macros/bg_core_pd/netlist/schematic/bg_core_pd_magic.spice`; **extracted** `macros/bg_core_pd/netlist/pex/bg_core_pd_magic_pex_3.spice` (magic PEX, R + C) |
| PDK | IHP-Open-PDK `ihp-sg13cmos5l`, commit `df4c5054ee8ca481c3eb873687274f28cdb50b1e`, `.spiceinit` of the PDK (OSDI psp103, r3_cmc, ...) |

## Tools

- `ngspice -v`: `ngspice-47 : Circuit level simulation program`, compiled with KLU (the decks use the default SPARSE
  solver).
- Python 3.13.18, matplotlib 3.11.2, numpy 2.5.3 (tables / plots only). CACE 2.11 installed, not used.

## Files

| file | what |
|---|---|
| `run.sh` | reproduces everything: `./run.sh [WORKDIR] [fetch\|designer\|modes\|bg\|tables]` (default all; at most 2 ngspice in parallel, set `NPAR` in the script) |
| `slot_modes.py` | slot 7 decks: `pu` = power-up transient per mode (the method of task 1), `dcop` = the designer's DC deck with stderr kept, `dcns` = `dcop` + `.nodeset` of the bandgap nodes (diagnosis only) |
| `jobs_modes.txt` | job list for `slot_modes.py`, priority order |
| `bg_core.py` | bg_core_pd alone: DC table, supply ramps, zero-state diagnosis |
| `tables.py`, `plots.py` | CSV / markdown tables, PNG plots from the runs |
| `designer_modes_rerun_ss.txt` | the designer's `run_modes.py`, unchanged, re-run at ss −40 °C here |
| `results.md` | verdict, summary, what it does not show |
| `results_tables.md` | all generated tables |
| `slot_modes_powerup.csv`, `slot_modes_dc_diag.csv` | slot 7 per mode × PVT; DC diagnosis |
| `bg_core_dc.csv`, `bg_core_ramp.csv`, `bg_core_diag.csv` | bg_core_pd alone |
| `slot_powerup_ss_m40.png`, `bg_core_ramp.png` | waveforms |

## Method

**Slot decks (`slot_modes.py pu`).** The designer's testbench (both pads, slot_7, pad in / pad out, bus sources and
loads as in `run_modes.py`), but as a transient from all nodes at 0 V (`uic`, no `.ic` / `.nodeset`):
vdd_3v3 and iovdd ramp 0 → V in 10 µs, v12 = 1.2 V and the mode bits from t = 0 (register written before enable),
`enable` 0 → 1.2 V at 20 µs. Pad in 0.5 V to 40 µs, then 0 / 0.25 / 0.5 / 0.75 / 1.0 V for 10 µs each, sampled
… [2883 more characters cut]
```

cd /home/user/sg13cmos5l_cm_slot7/verification/c2_gaps/slot_modes_bg && python3 - <<'EOF'
p='tables.py'; s=open(p).read()

new='''# ---------------- slot modes from power-up
pu = kv_rows(WORK / "slot_out" / "slot_modes_pu.csv") + kv_rows(WORK / "slot_out" / "slot_modes_pu0.csv")
seen = {}
for r in pu:  # last run of a job wins; rows without 'cload' are from runs before the 10 pF load was added (1 Gohm only)
    r["cload"] = r.get("cload") or "none"
    r["variant"] = "pu" if r["cload"] != "none" else "pu0"
    seen[(r["cload"], r["mode"], r["mos"], r["temp"], r["vdd"])] = r
pu = list(seen.values())
write(pu, OUT / "slot_modes_powerup.csv", "slot 7 modes from power-up (slot_modes.py 'pu' with 10 pF at pad out, 'pu0' "
      "1 Gohm only); bg_core_pd schematic, pads / pad_ref_rb / bias_dist_pd extracted. err_max_mV = max |pad out - "
      "expected| over the pad-in steps; t_* absolute (enable at 20 us)")
for cl, title in (("10p", "pad out 1 Gohm || 10 pF"), ("none", "pad out 1 Gohm only (as run_modes.py)")):
    pts = sorted({(r["mos"], r["temp"], r["vdd"]) for r in pu if r["cload"] == cl},
                 key=lambda p: (int(p[1]), p[0], float(p[2])))
    if not pts:
        continue
    md.append(f"### Slot 7 modes from power-up, {title}: max |D2S pad out - expected| [mV]\\n")
    md.append("IHP sg13cmos5l simulation; bg_core_pd schematic, both pads / pad_ref_rb / bias_dist_pd extracted, "
              "ls_up_fs / s7_ctrl source. Pad in 0 / 0.25 / 0.5 / 0.75 / 1.0 V (feed modes: 0.5 V). "
              "`FAILED` = the transient aborted (see slot_modes_powerup.csv / README); empty = not run in the time "
              "box.\\n")
    md.append("| MOS | T [C] | V | " + " | ".join(MODES) + " | vcm / vref [V] | bandgap g [V] | t(g 90 %) after "
              "enable [us] |")
    md.append("|---|---|---|" + "---|" * len(MODES) + "---|---|---|")
    for p in pts:
        cells, vv, gg = [], "", ""
        for m in MODES:
            r = seen.get((cl, m, *p))
            cells.append("" if r is None else str(r.get("err_max_mV", "")))
            if r is not None and fnum(r.get("vcm_V")) is not None and m != "ext" and not vv:
                vv = f"{float(r['vcm_V']):.3f} / {float(r['vref_V']):.3f}"
                gg = f"{float(r['v_g_V']):.3f}"
        tgs = [fnum(seen[(cl, m, *p)].get("t_g90_us")) for m in MODES if (cl, m, *p) in seen]
        tgs = [x - 20.0 for x in tgs if x is not None]
        tg = f"{min(tgs):.2f} ... {max(tgs):.2f}" if tgs else ""
        md.append(f"| {p[0]} | {p[1]} | {p[2]} | " + " | ".join(cells) + f" | {vv} | {gg} | {tg} |")
    md.append("")

'''
s=s[:a]+new+s[b:]
open(p,'w').write(s)
EOF

<!--

SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
-->
# Results: slot 7 modes at ss / −40 °C, mode table, bg_core_pd alone

IHP sg13cmos5l simulation, ngspice-47. Views: in the slot decks `bg_core_pd` **schematic**, both custom pads,
`pad_ref_rb`, `bias_dist_pd` **extracted** (layout netlists), `ls_up_fs` / `s7_ctrl` / `hv_logic` / `en_ctrl_3v3`
source; `bg_core_pd` alone: **schematic** and **extracted** (magic PEX R + C). Design repository commit `9052bd3`,
slot commit `158afc5`. Method and limits: `README.md`.

## 1. ss / −40 °C mode failures: simulator artefact, not a second state of the bandgap

**Verdict: simulator (DC solver / time-step) artefact.** In every run where the circuit was simulated from power-up,
the bandgap started and the slot settled to the expected output; no zero-current (second) state of `bg_core_pd` was
found by any method. Evidence:

1. **Reproduced.** The designer's `run_modes.py`, unchanged, at ss −40 °C 3.0 V: default and all_high pass (1.31 mV),
   swap, mon, feed, ext, swap+feed `FAILED` (`designer_modes_rerun_ss.txt`), as in the design repository.
2. **Where the solver fails.** ngspice's stderr for the same DC deck (`slot_modes_dc_diag.csv`): gmin stepping, source
   stepping and the "transient op" fallback all fail, the last with *timestep too small, trouble with
   dparea-instance* **`d.x2d.xlay.x$3.d1`** (swap; the D2S pad's antenna diode `dpantenna` pad → iovdd) or
   **`d.x2s.xlay.x$63.d1`** (feed, ext, swap+feed; the S2D pad's). Not a bandgap node.
3. **`.nodeset` of the bandgap at its running point does not cure it** (diagnosis only): mon then converges
   (1.37 mV, g = 1.7726 V), swap / feed / ext / swap+feed still fail at the same pad diodes. A DC failure caused by the
   bandgap sitting in a wrong state would be cured by placing it in the right one.
4. **Power-up transient from 0 V, all 8 modes at ss −40 °C 3.0 V** (supplies 0 → 3.0 V in 10 µs, mode bits set,
   enable at 20 µs; pad out 1 GΩ ∥ 10 pF): the bandgap's `g` node reaches 90 % **0.80 … 0.83 µs after enable** in
   every mode, vcm reaches 98 % in 1.6 µs (ext: vcm is external, 6 µs after its source switches on); settled values
   g = 1.7726 V, ibg node 0.995 V, vcm 1.1148 V, vref 0.4475 V, I(vdd_3v3) 48.38 … 48.59 µA — the same as the
   designer's DC at ss −40 °C (vcm 1.115, vref 0.447 V, 48.38 µA). Max |pad out − expected| over pad in 0 … 1 V:
   default / all_high / no_key_swap 1.37, swap 1.12, mon 1.43, feed 0.24, swap+feed 0.24, ext 4.92 mV (ext: against
   the source values 1.30 / 0.50 V through 10 kΩ). Plot: `slot_powerup_ss_m40.png`. With 1 GΩ only (as the designer)
   swap / mon / feed / default / all_high / no_key_swap also start and settle (same numbers); swap+feed aborted during
   the supply ramp (6.6 µs, before enable) at the D2S antenna diode.
5. **The aborts that remain are at edges, at the same pad diodes or a 0 V branch, and before or at enable:**
   1 GΩ only: 5.9 / 6.6 / 21.3 µs at `x2d … x$3` depending on options; with 10 pF, sf −40 °C mon at 20.07 µs (the
   enable edge, `x2d … x$3`), feed and swap+feed at 3.95 µs (supply ramp, `v.xs.vsb3#branch`), the bandgap not yet
   enabled in either case. Retried with a 1 µs enable edge and a 5 ns maximum step (column "retry").
6. **bg_core_pd alone** (schematic and extracted, all five MOS corners, −40 °C / 3.0 V and 125 °C / 3.6 V):
   `.op` started from a `.nodeset` at the zero-current state (g = 0, vpg = vpc = ks = vdd) converges to the running
   state (same current as the plain `.op`); a transient with the supply up and `.ic` at the zero-current state
   recovers within 0.02 µs (extracted ss −40 °C: 0.51 µs); 1 µs supply ramps reach 90 % of the final current
   1.4 … 2.4 µs after t = 0 and 1 ms ramps follow the supply with no hang (`bg_core_ramp.png`). The start-up
   transistor MS3 is off once running (ks ≈ 0 V in the slot at ss −40 °C).

## 2. Mode table

See the generated tables below (10 pF table = main result). Covered in

with the instance ngspice names. The ss −40 °C runs of section 1 of `results.md` were done in this order (pu0 first,
